## Method And Limits

- Emoji test: first non-space codepoint in the emoji blocks (U+1F000-1FAFF, U+2600-27BF, U+2300-23FF, U+2B00-2BFF, U+2194-21AA, U+25AA-25FE, plus common single codepoints). Keycap sequences (`#️⃣`, `1️⃣`) count as emoji starts.
- Docstrings: Rust `///` and `//!` groups (first line of the group), TS/Rust `/** */` blocks (first content line), Python `"""`/`'''` strings that open right after a `def`/`class` header or as the first statement of a module.
- Duplicate leading emoji: the same leading emoji sequence starting more than one docstring within one file; counts extra occurrences.
- Body comments: `//` line comments only (not `///`, `//!`); Rust/TS brace tracking skips strings and char literals, classifies a block by its header (`fn`, `impl`/`trait`/`class`/`interface`, other). Python `#` comments indented deeper than the enclosing `def`/`class`. `/* */` block comments are not counted.
- Heuristic brace parsing is approximate for TS template literals and regex literals; treat counts as an upper-bound audit list, not a parser-exact result.
